# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 006 PACKAGE INCIDENTS
# ============================================================================
# Two country incidents for each Level 2 package that had none: SCO, WLS, BRI,
# CAT, TRA, MAC, BOS, KAR, CRI, RUT, KUB, TAT, BSK, ARM, GEO, AZR, HAW, HBX, NAV,
# GLC and DON. The first is scheduled when the country settles its founding
# posture; either answer schedules the second. Each option trades the five
# Statehood values against the package's own ledgers, the former-host relation
# or a resource cost. Generation and due-date guards reject premature delayed
# calls. Each slot captures its shown generation/package on valid delivery,
# blocks replacement display until AFTER, and requeues only an existing current
# appointment against its original due date.
# ============================================================================

add_namespace = chaosx.nr6

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.nr6.101
	title = chaosx.nr6.101.t
	desc = chaosx.nr6.101.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_merchant_ship_01
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_independence_wave_sco_package = yes
		can_deliver_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
	}

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { independence_wave_package_incident_capture_a_shown = yes } }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.101.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.101.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double has_independence_wave_severe_host_threat = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_recognition_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_sco_shipping_delta = constant:independence_wave_scotland_wales_pressure.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_change_sco_shipping_authority = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_hostility_delta = constant:independence_wave_decision_effect.bilateral_minor_loss }
					independence_wave_decision_apply_host_deltas = yes
				}
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.101.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.101.b.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_constitutional_route = yes has_independence_wave_popular_council_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_recognition_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_security_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_instability_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_sco_shipping_delta = constant:independence_wave_scotland_wales_pressure.standard_gain }
				independence_wave_change_sco_shipping_authority = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_hostility_delta = constant:independence_wave_decision_effect.bilateral_minor }
					independence_wave_decision_apply_host_deltas = yes
				}
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}
	after = { independence_wave_package_incident_close_a_shown = yes }
}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.nr6.102
	title = chaosx.nr6.102.t
	desc = chaosx.nr6.102.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_gathering_protest
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_independence_wave_sco_package = yes
		can_deliver_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
	}

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { independence_wave_package_incident_capture_b_shown = yes } }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.102.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_political_power < constant:independence_wave_package_incident.political_power_requirement } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.102.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.high
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_constitutional_route = yes has_independence_wave_popular_council_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
				}
			}
			add_political_power = constant:independence_wave_package_incident.political_power_spend
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_b_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.standard_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_capacity_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_instability_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.102.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.102.b.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_emergency_military_route = yes has_independence_wave_severe_host_threat = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_b_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_security_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_instability_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_sco_shipping_delta = constant:independence_wave_scotland_wales_pressure.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_change_sco_shipping_authority = yes
			}
		}
	}
	after = { independence_wave_package_incident_close_b_shown = yes }
}

				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_recognition_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_instability_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_kc_return_delta = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_kc_settlement_delta = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_kc_forest_delta = constant:independence_wave_karelia_crimea_pressure.standard_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_kc_mandate_delta = 0 }
				independence_wave_change_kc_ledgers = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_hostility_delta = constant:independence_wave_decision_effect.bilateral_minor }
					independence_wave_decision_apply_host_deltas = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.139.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.high
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			add_political_power = constant:independence_wave_package_incident.political_power_spend
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_glc_council_delta = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_glc_port_delta = constant:independence_wave_iberian_pressure.standard_gain }
				independence_wave_change_glc_compact_values = yes
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}

			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
		}

					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}

		name = chaosx.nr6.140.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.140.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_constitutional_route = yes has_independence_wave_popular_council_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_b_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_recognition_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_glc_council_delta = constant:independence_wave_iberian_pressure.standard_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_glc_port_delta = 0 }
				independence_wave_change_glc_compact_values = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_hostility_delta = constant:independence_wave_decision_effect.bilateral_minor }
					independence_wave_decision_apply_host_deltas = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_b_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_capacity_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_instability_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_glc_council_delta = constant:independence_wave_iberian_pressure.minor_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_glc_port_delta = 0 }
				independence_wave_change_glc_compact_values = yes
			}
		}
	}
	after = { independence_wave_package_incident_close_b_shown = yes }

	desc = chaosx.nr6.204.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_independence_wave_imo_package = yes
		can_deliver_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
	}

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { independence_wave_package_incident_capture_a_shown = yes } }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.204.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.204.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_emergency_military_route = yes has_independence_wave_severe_host_threat = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_capacity_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.standard_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_instability_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.204.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.204.b.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_traditional_route = yes has_independence_wave_constitutional_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.standard_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_capacity_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}
	after = { independence_wave_package_incident_close_a_shown = yes }

	title = chaosx.nr6.221.t
	desc = chaosx.nr6.221.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_parliament
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_independence_wave_har_package = yes
		can_deliver_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
	}

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { independence_wave_package_incident_capture_b_shown = yes } }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.221.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.221.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.high
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_constitutional_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_b_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_recognition_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.221.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.221.b.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_traditional_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_b_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.standard_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_recognition_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
			}
		}
	}
	after = { independence_wave_package_incident_close_b_shown = yes }
}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.nr6.222
	title = chaosx.nr6.222.t
	desc = chaosx.nr6.222.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_merchant_ship_01
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_independence_wave_som_package = yes
		can_deliver_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
	}

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { independence_wave_package_incident_capture_a_shown = yes } }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.222.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.222.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_emergency_military_route = yes has_independence_wave_patron_client_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_capacity_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.standard_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.222.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.222.b.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_traditional_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.standard_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_capacity_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}
	after = { independence_wave_package_incident_close_a_shown = yes }
}

# Southern Africa and Indian Ocean framework packages (agent decision D29).

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.nr6.226
	title = chaosx.nr6.226.t
	desc = chaosx.nr6.226.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_merchant_ship_01
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_independence_wave_bar_package = yes
		can_deliver_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
	}

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { independence_wave_package_incident_capture_a_shown = yes } }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.226.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_political_power < constant:independence_wave_package_incident.political_power_requirement } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.226.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.high
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_traditional_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			add_political_power = constant:independence_wave_package_incident.political_power_spend
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.standard_gain }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.226.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.226.b.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_constitutional_route = yes has_independence_wave_patron_client_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_a = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_a_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_capacity_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
				independence_wave_schedule_package_incident_b = yes
			}
		}
	}
	after = { independence_wave_package_incident_close_a_shown = yes }
}

	}

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { independence_wave_package_incident_capture_b_shown = yes } }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.287.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_political_power < constant:independence_wave_package_incident.political_power_requirement } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.287.a.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.high
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
				}
			}
			add_political_power = constant:independence_wave_package_incident.political_power_spend
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_b_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_recognition_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.nr6.287.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.nr6.287.b.tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.standard
			modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_decision_ai.modifier_double OR = { has_independence_wave_emergency_military_route = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = independence_wave_incident_reply_pending_tt
					can_resolve_independence_wave_package_incident_b = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = independence_wave_package_incident_b_resolved
				independence_wave_package_incident_clear_deltas = yes
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_capacity_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_gain }
				set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_decision_legitimacy_delta = constant:independence_wave_value.minor_loss }
				independence_wave_decision_apply_country_deltas = yes
			}
		}
	}
	after = { independence_wave_package_incident_close_b_shown = yes }
